A live surface is versioned constitutional history, not scratch space.\n"},{"number":3056,"text":"\n"},{"number":3057,"text":"- Engine code, design documents, tests, and tooling may be refactored aggressively while they remain mutable implementation surfaces.\n"},{"number":3058,"text":"- Live domain artifacts, once published as live constitutional surfaces, are immutable in place.\n"},{"number":3059,"text":"- If a live domain artifact is wrong, the valid actions are:\n"},{"number":3060,"text":"  - supersede it with a new version, or\n"},{"number":3061,"text":"  - withdraw/delete it from the live surface.\n"},{"number":3062,"text":"- Transitional implementation paths, migration scaffolds, and fallback behaviors have no permanent authority in the live surface. Once superseded, they are retired; any retained responsibility satisfies the Core Interface Migration Rule's retention relation.\n"},{"number":3063,"text":"\n"},{"number":3064,"text":"The past is preserved by version control, operational event history where present, and superseded constitutional artifacts. Spec-driven development does not require shipping compatibility shims forever, but it also does not allow silent mutation of live constitutional history.\n"},{"number":3065,"text":"\n"},{"number":3066,"text":"---\n"},{"number":3067,"text":"\n"}]},{"end_line":3121,"heading":"Spec-Driven Homeostatic Methodology/Fundamental Migration Rule","id":"repo://stdo-v3-generation-rc9/codex/5592168e26759f37e730aed12c030a6061709ec4/source/standards/SPEC_METHOD.md#Spec-Driven%20Homeostatic%20Methodology%2FFundamental%20Migration%20Rule","path":"standards/SPEC_METHOD.md","sha256":"63c9e1cb6145bc66843081d66f94a255bd95b1e448f99842b31ce89e1746940f","start_line":3068,"source_file":"/Users/jim/src/apps/specification_methodology/a_c/dogfood/stdo-v3-generation-rc9/runs-v2/codex/graphify-ac/corpus/standards/SPEC_METHOD.md","lines":[{"number":3068,"text":"## Fundamental Migration Rule\n"},{"number":3069,"text":"\n"},{"number":3070,"text":"A fundamental migration is a constitutional reset, not an in-place refactor.\n"},{"number":3071,"text":"\n"},{"number":3072,"text":"This rule applies when a project intentionally abandons a prior constitutional shape and re-derives itself on a new branch, version line, or methodology basis.\n"},{"number":3073,"text":"\n"},{"number":3074,"text":"In that case:\n"},{"number":3075,"text":"\n"},{"number":3076,"text":"- inherited constitutional documents from the prior line become migration source material, not live authority in the new line\n"},{"number":3077,"text":"- it is lawful to zero inherited constitutional surfaces on the migration line and rebuild them from first principles\n"},{"number":3078,"text":"- nothing carries forward by default; every retained statement, requirement, design choice, or guide rule must be explicitly re-adopted\n"},{"number":3079,"text":"- every carry-forward is intentional and should land in the correct layer: goals, intent, product, requirements, design, guide, or template\n"},{"number":3080,"text":"- old design and code may be used as reference implementations, but they do not govern the new constitutional surface unless explicitly re-derived and re-adopted\n"},{"number":3081,"text":"- absence from the new constitutional surface means \"not yet adopted\" and carries no automatic authority from the prior line\n"},{"number":3082,"text":"- the prior implementation may be moved sideways as a reference line, but that\n"},{"number":3083,"text":"  sideways line has no live implementation authority in the new line\n"},{"number":3084,"text":"- every inherited module, interface, carrier, projection, and proof surface must\n"},{"number":3085,"text":"  be explicitly classified before reuse\n"},{"number":3086,"text":"\n"},{"number":3087,"text":"The required implementation classifications are:\n"},{"number":3088,"text":"\n"},{"number":3089,"text":"- `carry_across`: the module remains materially the same and is intentionally\n"},{"number":3090,"text":"  re-adopted into the new line\n"},{"number":3091,"text":"- `redundant`: the module is no longer needed in the new line\n"},{"number":3092,"text":"- `rewrite`: the module remains needed but must be rebuilt under the new\n"},{"number":3093,"text":"  requirements and design\n"},{"number":3094,"text":"\n"},{"number":3095,"text":"Unclassified inherited implementation is a defect.\n"},{"number":3096,"text":"\n"},{"number":3097,"text":"The purpose of this rule is to prevent accidental law from leaking through migration by mere inheritance. Fundamental migration is controlled adoption, not passive preservation.\n"},{"number":3098,"text":"\n"},{"number":3099,"text":"When performing a fundamental migration:\n"},{"number":3100,"text":"\n"},{"number":3101,"text":"1. Declare the migration line and treat the prior line as source material.\n"},{"number":3102,"text":"2. Establish fresh constitutional surfaces for method, goals, intent, product, requirements, and design.\n"},{"number":3103,"text":"3. Move the prior implementation sideways if needed so it cannot continue as ambient live authority.\n"},{"number":3104,"text":"4. Classify inherited constitutional material as adopted, superseded, deferred, or orphaned.\n"},{"number":3105,"text":"5. Classify inherited implementation material as `carry_across`, `redundant`, or `rewrite`.\n"},{"number":3106,"text":"6. Copy forward only what is intentionally retained.\n"},{"number":3107,"text":"7. Re-derive downstream design and code from the new constitutional surfaces, not from ambient precedent.\n"},{"number":3108,"text":"\n"},{"number":3109,"text":"For a fundamental migration:\n"},{"number":3110,"text":"\n"},{"number":3111,"text":"- `carry_across` does not mean automatic copy-forward; it means explicit\n"},{"number":3112,"text":"  re-adoption under the new line\n"},{"number":3113,"text":"- direct runtime, projection, prompt, proof, or review dependence on the\n"},{"number":3114,"text":"  sideways implementation line is bridge debt\n"},{"number":3115,"text":"- proxy interfaces that partially imitate the target line while still depending\n"},{"number":3116,"text":"  on the sideways line are not lawful closure\n"},{"number":3117,"text":"\n"},{"number":3118,"text":"This is a lawful form of supersession, not a violation of live-surface immutability, because the new line is creating a new constitutional surface rather than silently mutating the old one.\n"},{"number":3119,"text":"\n"},{"number":3120,"text":"---\n"},{"number":3121,"text":"\n"}]},{"end_line":3160,"heading":"Spec-Driven It shall not carry optional realization detail unless that detail is itself constitutional.\n"},{"number":3203,"text":"- **Product** states the current concrete product definition and bridges intent to requirements. It is not a release note surface. It shall carry enough operational lifecycle signal for downstream requirements and design to understand intended use, release/install posture, live-use posture, observability posture, and retirement or supersession posture where those materially affect product meaning.\n"},{"number":3204,"text":"- **Requirements** state stable obligations that must be true. They are not a second requirement surface.\n"},{"number":3207,"text":"\n"},{"number":3208,"text":"Constitutional specification is current-surface law and shall be written in present tense.\n"},{"number":3209,"text":"\n"},{"number":3210,"text":"Dead law shall not remain in active constitutional artifacts for historical comfort. If something is no longer live, it shall be deleted, superseded, or moved to commentary. A migration wave does not justify preserving stale constitutional wording inside the live surface.\n"},{"number":3211,"text":"\n"},{"number":3212,"text":"During a change wave, write the live constitutional surface in terms of the current truth and the declared affected boundary. Neither Foundations nor adoption requires twelve\n"},{"number":839,"text":"actors or components.\n"},{"number":840,"text":"\n"},{"number":841,"text":"This profile declares the following generic frame families for Product work.\n"},{"number":842,"text":"Each row is an evaluation archetype under `REFERENCE_FRAME_METHOD.md`, not a\n"},{"number":843,"text":"complete frame declaration. An adopting project binds the exact subject,\n"},{"number":844,"text":"basis, material manifold, coordinates, invariants, authority, evidence,\n"},{"number":845,"text":"exclusions, result algebra, capability envelope, dependencies, and\n"},{"number":846,"text":"invalidation conditions for every activated instance.\n"},{"number":847,"text":"\n"},{"number":848,"text":"The family labels resolve inside\n"},{"number":849,"text":"`urn:stdo:bounded-context:reference-frame-evaluation` under the selected STDO\n"},{"number":850,"text":"basis. Under `SPEC_METHOD.md`'s Bounded-Context Semantic Resolution law,\n"},{"number":851,"text":"`Product`, `Owner`, `Entity`, `Operator`, `Install`, and the other labels do not\n"},{"number":852,"text":"become equal to same-spelled Product, authority, domain, or runtime concepts.\n"},{"number":853,"text":"An explicit relation is required at every material cross-context seam.\n"},{"number":854,"text":"\n"},{"number":855,"text":"| Frame family | Evaluation family and exact subject | Governing authority and permitted output | Immediate falsifier |\n"},{"number":856,"text":"|---|---|---|---|\n"},{"number":857,"text":"| **Product** | Does one exact current Product claim, outcome, boundary, and lifecycle govern the claimed Product subject without downstream reinterpretation? The basis is current Intent, Product, requirements, acceptance meaning, and exact Product coordinates. | Product and requirement authority retain meaning and acceptance. The frame returns only a bounded evaluation result and evidence. | Design, code, tests, a predecessor behavior, or local convention changes Product meaning or substitutes a different Product subject. |\n"},{"number":858,"text":"| **Product Composition** | Does one exact directed relation between Products preserve the source and target roles, Product Definition, Release Cut, Product, and Install identities, each Development Product role binding, governing contracts, authority, lifecycle, evidence boundary, and invalidation law? A chain is evaluated through bounded edge activations and declared conjunction. | Each Product retains its own meaning. The authority and contracts cited by the composition relation own the edge. The frame returns evaluation evidence only. | Directory nesting, shared implementation, common actor, source import, tool reachability, matching output, or builder convenience creates or changes a composition relation; a Development Product is confused with the Product being authored; or one node silently redefines another. |\n"},{"number":859,"text":"| **Design** | Does the accepted structural `HOW` satisfy the material constitutional `WHAT`, close the required design relations, and preserve their owners across the affected topology? | Accepted design under its governing Product and requirements owns structural decisions. The frame may identify satisfaction, contradiction, or a design re-entry gap; it cannot invent Product truth. | Implementation precedent resolves an open material design decision, design creates Product meaning, or a material relation or competing structure remains undeclared. |\n"},{"number":860,"text":"| **Design Component** | Does one bounded design component and its direct causal dependency cone preserve its parent contract, internal relations, sibling seams, and required outputs? | The accepted design boundary owns only its bounded composition decision. Parent, sibling, Product, requirement, and runtime owners retain their relations. | The component omits a material far-side seam, absorbs a sibling or parent decision, or reports local closure while its direct composition is unresolved. |\n"},{"number":861,"text":"| **Public Boundary** | Does one supported public entry or exit perform exactly its assigned validation, selection, transport, refusal, and projection relations while preserving the selected interior owners? | Product, interface, identity, security, and accepted-design authority define the boundary. The frame evaluates the boundary result or refusal and returns only its closed evaluation; it does not recreate interior semantics. | The public surface interprets owner meaning, sequences hidden domain decisions, exposes an interior carrier as Product truth, or admits an unsupported path. |\n"},{"number":862,"text":"| **Entity** | Is one semantic, artifact, or runtime entity bound to its exact identity, kind, lifecycle, owner, representation, and applicable effect regime? | Product, domain, identity, requirement, and accepted-design authority define the entity. The frame returns an identity- and lifecycle-bound evaluation. | A filename, wrapper, ticket, object shape, cache, or process instance substitutes for stable entity identity, or distinct lifecycle coordinates are collapsed. |\n"},{"number":863,"text":"| **Operator** | Is one material operation an irreducible declared relation over its admitted domain, inputs, outputs, refusals, algebra, effects, and lawful compositions? | Product, requirements, accepted Ontology and design, and the named operation owner define the relation. The frame evaluates it without creating another executable plan. | An endpoint, function, class, command, or file label is asserted to be the semantic atom; orchestration hides the real operator; or generic mechanics are rebuilt as domain law. |\n"},{"number":864,"text":"| **Owner** | Does one semantic or authority-bearing relation preserve its owner, role kind, subject, scope, basis, lifecycle, refusal invariants, and permitted delegation? | The existing Product, requirement, identity, accepted-design, release, or other named authority owns the relation. The frame may evaluate authority congruence only. | Another surface recreates the judgment, source authorship is treated as authority transfer, or a proxy, foundation, frame, or actor widens the grant. |\n"},{"number":865,"text":"| **Effect** | Does one action obey its declared pure, observational, artifact, or runtime effect regime, including commit, failure, retry, recovery, compensation, admission, and projection law where material? | The applicable Product, requirement, design, artifact, runtime, and admission owners retain their decisions. The frame returns an effect-bound result and evidence. | Distinct effect regimes are collapsed, a mutation bypasses its admitted owner, compensation is treated as semantic rollback, or a read becomes rival writable truth. |\n"},{"number":866,"text":"| **Reuse/Foundation** | Has one material generic capability or dependency been normalized and compared against credible lawful foundations with any irreducible local remainder and authority-bearing role preserved? | `DESIGN_MODULE_METHOD.md` `STDO-UP-023` and the existing tradeoff owner govern selection. The frame cannot transfer semantic authority to a foundation. | Incumbent or local code wins without bounded comparison, a richer lawful foundation is reproduced, unknowns are scored favorably, or reuse acquires owner authority. |\n"},{"number":867,"text":"| **Install** | Is the exact selected Product, release, artifact, dependency set, configuration, and callable entry resolved and exercised through its admitted installed coordinate independently of mutable source convenience? | Product, identity, release, dependency, packaging, and accepted-design authority define the installed subject. The frame returns installed identity, reachability, and refusal evidence. | Source-tree imports, ambient resolution, process registries, fixture bindings, rebuilt artifacts, or a moving workspace substitute for the claimed installed subject. |\n"},{"number":868,"text":"| **Proof** | Does one exact claim have congruent subject, basis, path, population, oracle, evidence, falsifier, residual boundary, and invalidation law? | The claim owner defines meaning; applicable testing, assurance, review, Product, and release owners define evaluation and acceptance. The frame returns evidence and a closed evaluation result, not Product meaning or disposition. | A harness recreates owner meaning, evidence targets another subject or path, count or aggregate green substitutes for the claim, or proof tooling mutates the subject undetectably. |\n"},{"number":869,"text":"\n"},{"number":870,"text":"These families are non-hierarchical and may overlap. Product does not outrank\n"},{"number":871,"text":"Design by frame topology; Product authority already governs constitutional\n"},{"number":872,"text":"meaning. Owner does not gain authority over Entity or Operator by being a\n"},{"number":873,"text":"broader frame. Proof does not become Reviewer, acceptance, or disposition\n"},{"number":874,"text":"authority. The existing authority graph remains controlling.\n"},{"number":875,"text":"\n"},{"number":876,"text":"For each governed outcome, the known evaluation inventory dispositions every\n"},{"number":877,"text":"family as one of:\n"},{"number":878,"text":"\n"},{"number":879,"text":"- instantiated by one or more exact frame declarations or authoritative\n"},{"number":880,"text":"  acquisition paths because the family is material;\n"},{"number":881,"text":"- evidenced as non-material to the exact outcome by the authority capable of\n"},{"number":882,"text":"  making that determination; or\n"},{"number":883,"text":"- retained as an explicit uncovered, conditional, conflicted, or\n"},{"number":884,"text":"  scope/capability residual (with any actual `out_of_frame` evaluation result\n"},{"number":885,"text":"  referenced separately).\n"},{"number":886,"text":"\n"},{"number":887,"text":"These are inventory treatments, not alternate evaluation verdicts. When\n"},{"number":888,"text":"evaluating “every family is accounted for on this outcome and basis”, supported\n"},{"number":889,"text":"material instantiation, supported non-materiality or an explicit justified\n"},{"number":890,"text":"residual satisfies that family's accounting predicate. An established omission\n"},{"number":891,"text":"or disproved treatment defeats it (`falsified`); undecided treatment or missing\n"},{"number":892,"text":"reason is `indeterminate`; an unusable basis is `invalid_basis`; evaluation\n"},{"number":893,"text":"scope/capability outside the frame is `out_of_frame`. Apply core conjunction to\n"},{"number":894,"text":"the required family-accounting predicates and retain performance and effects.\n"},{"number":895,"text":"Accounting for a residual does not establish adequate coverage or the family's\n"},{"number":896,"text":"underlying Product claim. Conversely, adopting this profile does not\n"},{"number":902,"text":"require a project to create twelve components or activate twelve frames for\n"},{"number":903,"text":"every task.\n"},{"number":904,"text":"\n"},{"number":905,"text":"When a generic frame is recursively refined, each child declaration records\n"},{"number":906,"text":"its parent contract, purpose, material entities, identity and lifecycle\n"},{"number":907,"text":"relations, semantic owners, direct sibling seams, applicable invariants, and\n"},{"number":908,"text":"prohibited competing paths. Cross-frame values, evidence\n"},{"number":915,"text":"and operations use the other [frame relations](REFERENCE_FRAME_METHOD.md#using-frames-in-conjunction)\n"},{"number":916,"text":"where needed. It is not indexed by test, assertion, file,\n"},{"number":1089,"text":"line, branch, mock, snapshot, or runner count.\n"},{"number":1090,"text":"\n"},{"number":1091,"text":"For one claim, the testing-value index considers at least:\n"},{"number":1092,"text":"\n"},{"number":1093,"text":"1. congruence between the claim and evaluated subject;\n"},{"number":1094,"text":"2. the number and materiality of independent claim dimensions;\n"},{"number":1095,"text":"3. the interaction strength actually covered among those dimensions;\n"},{"number":1096,"text":"4. authoritative-path exactness;\n"},{"number":1097,"text":"5. oracle independence and precision;\n"},{"number":1098,"text":"6. sensitivity to realistic faults, rival paths, and historical failures;\n"},{"number":1099,"text":"7. observability, reconstruction, and reproducibility of the result; and\n"},{"number":1100,"text":"8. explicit residual combinations and invalidation conditions.\n"},{"number":1101,"text":"\n"},{"number":1102,"text":"No universal scalar is required.
